        self.termination()  # PERFORM 9000-TERMINATION

    def initialization(self):

        self.validate_system()  # PERFORM 1500-VALIDATE-SYSTEM
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED")  # DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED".

@dataclass

def calculate_premiums():
    """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
                PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
                PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    insurance_master_list = [InsuranceMaster(),InsuranceMaster(INS_HEALTH = True),InsuranceMaster(INS_AUTO = True),InsuranceMaster(INS_HOME = True),InsuranceMaster(INS_UMBRELLA = True)] #dummy data
    insurance_index = 0
    while not WS_EOF:
        if insurance_index < len(insurance_master_list):
            insurance_master = insurance_master_list[insurance_index]
            determine_base_premium(insurance_master)
            apply_risk_factor(insurance_master)
            calculate_final_premium(insurance_master)
            insurance_index += 1
        else:
            WS_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False # reset WS_EOF for future use

def determine_base_premium(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN INS-LIFE
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
        WHEN INS-HEALTH
            MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
        WHEN INS-AUTO
            MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
        WHEN INS-HOME
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
        WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
            MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
    END-EVALUATE."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    match True:
        case insurance_master.INS_LIFE:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.INS_HEALTH:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.INS_AUTO:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.INS_HOME:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.INS_UMBRELLA:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
    IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
        COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
    END-IF."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

def calculate_final_premium(insurance_master: InsuranceMaster):
    """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM.
    MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO INS-PREMIUM-AMOUNT
    ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-PREMIUMS."""
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    insurance_master.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE
                PERFORM 5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS
                PERFORM 5230-UPDATE-TOTALS
        END-READ

    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    investment_master_list = [InvestmentMaster(),InvestmentMaster(INV_STOCKS = True),InvestmentMaster(INV_BONDS = True),InvestmentMaster(INV_MUTUAL_FUND = True)] #dummy data
    investment_index = 0

    while not WS_EOF:
        if investment_index < len(investment_master_list):
            investment_master = investment_master_list[investment_index]
            calculate_position_value(investment_master)
            calculate_gain_loss(investment_master)
            update_totals(investment_master)

def calculate_position_value(investment_master: InvestmentMaster):
    """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
    COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
        INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE."""
    investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE = investment_master.INV_QUANTITY * investment_master.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss(investment_master: InvestmentMaster):
    """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
    COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
        INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE)."""
    investment_master.INV_GAIN_LOSS = investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (investment_master.INV_QUANTITY * investment_master.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def update_totals(investment_master: InvestmentMaster):
    """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS.
    ADD INV-MARKET-VALUE TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE

def calculate_dividends():
    """5400-CALCULATE-DIVIDENDS.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                IF INV-DIVIDEND-RATE > 0
                    PERFORM 5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND
                    PERFORM 5420-POST-DIVIDEND
                END-IF
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    investment_master_list = [InvestmentMaster(INV_DIVIDEND_RATE = Decimal('0.01')),InvestmentMaster()] #dummy data
    investment_index = 0
    while not WS_EOF:
        if investment_index < len(investment_master_list):
            investment_master = investment_master_list[investment_index]
            if investment_master.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                compute_dividend(investment_master)
                post_dividend()
            investment_index += 1
        else:
            WS_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False # reset WS_EOF for future use

def format_date():
    """8300-FORMAT-DATE.
    STRING WS-TEMP-DATE(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-TEMP-DATE(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE

           INTO WS-FORMATTED-DATE."""

def calculate_tax():
    """8500-CALCULATE-TAX.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
        WHEN OTHER
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
    END-EVALUATE."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    match True:
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def termination():
    """9000-TERMINATION.
    PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
    PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS

def fraud_detection():
    """7000-FRAUD-DETECTION.
    PERFORM 7100-ANALYZE-PATTERNS
    PERFORM 7200-CHECK-VELOCITY
    PERFORM 7300-GEOGRAPHIC-ANALYSIS
    PERFORM 7400-BEHAVIORAL-SCORING
    PERFORM 7500-ALERT-GENERATION."""
    analyze_patterns()
    check_velocity()
    geographic_analysis()
    behavioral_scoring()
    alert_generation()

def analyze_patterns():
    """7100-ANALYZE-PATTERNS.
    DISPLAY "ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 7110-CHECK-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
                PERFORM 7120-CHECK-FREQUENCY
                PERFORM 7130-CHECK-TIME-PATTERN
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    transaction_log_list = [TransactionLog(TRAN_AMOUNT = Decimal('10001.00')),TransactionLog(TRAN_AMOUNT = Decimal('5000.00'))] #dummy data
    transaction_index = 0

    while not WS_EOF:
        if transaction_index < len(transaction_log_list):
            transaction_log = transaction_log_list[transaction_index]
            check_amount_threshold(transaction_log)
            check_frequency()
            check_time_pattern()
            transaction_index += 1
        else:
            WS_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False # reset WS_EOF for future use

def check_amount_threshold(transaction_log: TransactionLog):
    """7110-CHECK-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD.
    IF TRAN-AMOUNT > 10000
        PERFORM 7115-FLAG-LARGE-TRANSACTION
    END-IF."""
    if transaction_log.TRAN_AMOUNT > 10000:
        flag_large_transaction()

def flag_large_transaction():
    """7115-FLAG-LARGE-TRANSACTION.
    ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT
    PERFORM 8200-WRITE-AUDIT."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
    write_audit()

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    """Record structure for CUSTOMER-MASTER."""
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_last_activity: int = 0
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.cust_total_balance = Decimal(self.cust_total_balance)
        self.cust_total_loans = Decimal(self.cust_total_loans)
        self.cust_total_investments = Decimal(self.cust_total_investments)

@dataclass
class Account:
    """Record structure for Account."""
    acct_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.acct_balance = Decimal(self.acct_balance)
        self.acct_min_balance = Decimal(self.acct_min_balance)

    def customer_segmentation(self):
        """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
        print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            self.read_customer_master()
            if not self.ws_eof:
                self.calculate_clv()
                self.assign_segment()

    def read_customer_master(self):
        """Simulates reading the next customer record."""
        # Replace with actual data reading logic

        # if data_available():
        #   self.customer_master = get_next_customer()

    def calculate_clv(self):
        """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
        self.ws_calc_result = (self.customer_master.cust_total_balance * self.ws_savings_rate) + \
                               (self.customer_master.cust_total_loans * self.ws_personal_rate) + \
                               (self.customer_master.cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

    def assign_segment(self):
        """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
        if self.ws_calc_result > 10000:
            self.ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
        elif self.ws_calc_result > 5000:
            self.ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
        elif self.ws_calc_result > 1000:
            self.ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
        else:
            self.ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

    def sweep_accounts(self):
        """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
        if self.account.acct_balance > self.account.acct_min_balance:
            self.ws_calc_amount = self.account.acct_balance - self.account.acct_min_balance
            self.account.acct_balance -= self.ws_calc_amount
            self.ws_total_investments += self.ws_calc_amount

    def extract_data(self):
        """A110-EXTRACT-DATA."""
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            self.read_customer_master()
            if not self.ws_eof:
                self.ws_process_count += 1

    def transform_data(self):
        """A120-TRANSFORM-DATA."""
        self.cleanse_data()
        self.standardize_data()
        self.enrich_data()

        """A121-CLEANSE-DATA."""
        if self.customer_master.cust_name == "":
            self.customer_master.cust_last_name = "UNKNOWN"

        self.timeliness_check()

    def completeness_check(self):
        """A210-COMPLETENESS-CHECK."""
        if self.customer_master.cust_id == "":
            self.ws_error_count += 1

    def accuracy_check(self):
        """A220-ACCURACY-CHECK."""
        if self.customer_master.cust_credit_score < 300 or self.customer_master.cust_credit_score > 850:
            self.ws_error_count += 1

    def consistency_check(self):
        """A230-CONSISTENCY-CHECK."""
        pass

    def timeliness_check(self):

        if self.customer_master.cust_last_activity < self.ws_current_date - 365:
            pass

def c100_transaction_monitoring(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        #READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
        #    AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
        #    NOT AT END
        if data.TRANSACTION_LOG:
            c110_rule_based_detection(data)
            c120_behavior_analysis(data)
            c130_network_analysis(data)
        else:
            data.WS_EOF = True
           
def c110_rule_based_detection(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if data.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr(data)
    if 5000 <= data.TRAN_AMOUNT < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring(data)
           
def c111_flag_ctr(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    data.WS_PROCESS-COUNT += 1

def d110_classification(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A'
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B'
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C'
    else:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D'
           
def d120_regression(data: DataFields) -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + \
                           (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - \
                           (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000)

    def main_control(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            self.process_transactions()
        self.finalization()
    
    def read_customer_master(self):
        """Helper function for reading customer master file."""
        # Replace this with actual file reading logic.
        try:
            self.CUSTOMER_MASTER = CustomerMaster() # read from CUSTOMER-MASTER
            return True # successful read
        except:
            return False # failed to read

    def process_customer_master(self):
        """Main loop for processing customer master records."""
        """
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM I110-UPDATE-PROFILE
                       PERFORM I120-ENRICH-PROFILE
                       ADD 1 TO WS-CUST-COUNT
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        self.WS_EOF = False
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            if self.read_customer_master():
                self.i110_update_profile()
                self.i120_enrich_profile()
                self.WS_CUST_COUNT += 1
            else:
                self.WS_EOF = True

    def i110_update_profile(self):
        """I110-UPDATE-PROFILE.
           MOVE WS-CURRENT-DATE TO CUST-LAST-ACTIVITY.
        """
        self.CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY = self.WS_CURRENT_DATE

    def initialization(self):
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.WS_WORK_AREAS = None #Dummy init
        self.WS_COUNTERS = None    #Dummy init
        self.WS_TOTALS = None      #Dummy init
        self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = "20240101000000" #Dummy date
        self.WS_CURR_YEAR  = self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME[:4]
        self.WS_CURR_MONTH = self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME[4:6]
        self.WS_CURR_DAY   = self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME[6:8]
        self.rpt_year = self.WS_CURR_YEAR
        self.rpt_month = self.WS_CURR_MONTH
        self.rpt_day = self.WS_CURR_DAY
        self.open_files()
        self.read_parameters()
        self.initialize_tables()
        self.load_reference_data()

    def open_files(self):
        """1100-OPEN-FILES."""
        self.CUSTOMER_FILE = None #Dummy init
        self.ACCOUNT_FILE  = None #Dummy init
        self.TRANSACTION_FILE = None #Dummy init
        self.REPORT_FILE = None #Dummy init
        self.ERROR_FILE = None #Dummy init
        self.MASTER_FILE = None #Dummy init

        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
            self.abort_process()

    def read_parameters(self):
        """1200-READ-PARAMETERS."""
        self.WS_PARAM_DATE = "20240101" #Dummy date
        self.WS_PARAM_TIME = "000000" #Dummy time
        self.WS_JOB_ID = 'BATCH-001'
        self.WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
        self.WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(self.WS_PARAM_DATE)

    def initialize_tables(self):
        """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
            self.RATE_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = RateTableEntry()
            self.RATE_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00")
            self.RATE_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_CODE = ""

        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
            self.BRANCH_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = BranchTableEntry()

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
        self.WS_TBL_IDX = 1
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and self.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
            if self.read_reference_file():
                self.RATE_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_CODE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE
                self.RATE_TABLE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_RATE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE
                self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def read_reference_file(self) -> bool:
        """Helper function to simulate reading from REFERENCE-FILE."""
        # Replace with actual file reading logic. Return True if read, False if AT END.
        try:
            self.WS_REF_RECORD = ReferenceFileRecord(WS_REF_CODE=f"CODE{self.WS_TBL_IDX}", WS_REF_RATE=Decimal(str(self.WS_TBL_IDX * 1.1)))
            return True
        except:
            return False

    def process_transactions(self):
        """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
        if self.read_transaction_file():
            self.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
            self.validate_transaction()
            if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
                self.process_by_type()
            else:
                self.handle_error()
        else:
            self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    def read_transaction_file(self) -> bool:
        """Helper function to simulate reading from TRANSACTION-FILE."""
        # Replace with actual file reading logic.  Return True if read, False if AT END.
        try:
            # Simulate a new transaction record
            self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC = TransactionRec(
                TXN_ACCOUNT_ID=f"ACCT{self.WS_TRANS_COUNT}",
                TXN_AMOUNT=Decimal(str(self.WS_TRANS_COUNT * 10.5)),
                TXN_TYPE=['D', 'W', 'T', 'I'][self.WS_TRANS_COUNT % 4],
                TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT=f"TGT{self.WS_TRANS_COUNT}"
            )
            self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
            self.TXN_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT
            self.TXN_TYPE = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE
            self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT

            return True  # Successful read
        except Exception:
            return False  # End of file or error
    
    def validate_transaction(self):
        """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
        if self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
            return
        try:
            Decimal(self.TXN_AMOUNT)
        except:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
            return

        if self.TXN_TYPE not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
        self.validate_account_exists()
        self.validate_business_rules()

    def validate_account_exists(self):
        """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
        self.search_account()
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

    def validate_business_rules(self):
        """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
        if self.TXN_TYPE == 'W':
            if self.TXN_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
                self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
        if self.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal("1000000.00"):
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

    def process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.TXN_TYPE:
            case 'D':
                self.process_deposit()
            case 'W':
                self.process_withdrawal()
            case 'T':
                self.process_transfer()
            case 'I':
                self.process_interest()
            case _:
                self.handle_error()

    def process_deposit(self):
        """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'
        self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += self.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1
        self.update_account()
        self.write_audit_trail()

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "20240101" #Dummy date

        if self.rewrite_account_record(): #Simulating file update
            if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'
                self.handle_error()

    def rewrite_account_record(self) -> bool:
        """Dummy function to simulate file rewrite."""
        # Simulates rewriting the ACCOUNT-RECORD.
        # Replace with actual file I/O.
        # Returns True for success, False for failure.
        return True

    def write_audit_trail(self):
        """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WSAuditRecord()
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_AMOUNT = self.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TYPE = self.TXN_TYPE
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "20240101" #Dummy date
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_JOB_ID = self.WS_JOB_ID

        # Simulate writing to the AUDIT-RECORD.
        # Replace with actual file I/O.
        self.AUDIT_RECORD = self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD

    def process_withdrawal(self):
        """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'
        self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += self.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1
        self.update_account()
        self.write_audit_trail()
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < self.WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:
            self.generate_low_balance_alert()

    def generate_low_balance_alert(self):
        """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD = AlertRecord()
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_TYPE = 'LOW-BAL'
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_DATE = "20240101"  # Dummy date

        # Simulate writing to the ALERT-RECORD.
        # Replace with actual file I/O.
        self.ALERT_RECORD = self.WS_ALERT_RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1

    def process_transfer(self):
        """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_target_account()
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            self.debit_source()
            self.credit_target()
            self.record_transfer()
        else:
            self.handle_error()

# === CHUNK 6 (lines 3341-4008) ===
def set_interest_rate(interest_rate: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """
    COBOL: EVALUATE TRUE
    """
    match True:
        case _: # COBOL: WHEN OTHER
            interest_rate = Decimal('2.5') # COBOL: MOVE 2.5 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
    return interest_rate # COBOL: END-EVALUATE.

def calculate_simple_interest(account_balance: Decimal, interest_rate: Decimal, days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """7200-CALCULATE-SIMPLE-INTEREST."""
    simple_interest = (account_balance * interest_rate * days_in_period) / Decimal('36500') # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-SIMPLE-INTEREST = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE * WS-DAYS-IN-PERIOD / 36500.
    return simple_interest

def calculate_compound_interest(account_balance: Decimal, interest_rate: Decimal, days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """7300-CALCULATE-COMPOUND-INTEREST."""
    compound_factor = (1 + interest_rate / Decimal('36500')) ** days_in_period # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-COMPOUND-FACTOR = (1 + WS-INTEREST-RATE / 36500) ** WS-DAYS-IN-PERIOD
    compound_interest = account_balance * (compound_factor - 1) # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-COMPOUND-INTEREST = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * (WS-COMPOUND-FACTOR - 1).
    return compound_interest

def apply_interest(interest_method: str, simple_interest: Decimal, compound_interest: Decimal, account_balance: Decimal, update_account) -> Decimal:
    """7400-APPLY-INTEREST."""
    if interest_method == 'S': # COBOL: IF WS-INTEREST-METHOD = 'S'
        account_balance += simple_interest # COBOL: ADD WS-SIMPLE-INTEREST TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    else: # COBOL: ELSE
        account_balance += compound_interest # COBOL: ADD WS-COMPOUND-INTEREST TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account() # COBOL: PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
    return account_balance

def fee_processing(calculate_monthly_fee, calculate_transaction_fees, apply_fee_waivers, deduct_fees):
    """8000-FEE-PROCESSING."""
    calculate_monthly_fee() # COBOL: PERFORM 8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE
    calculate_transaction_fees() # COBOL: PERFORM 8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES
    apply_fee_waivers() # COBOL: PERFORM 8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS
    deduct_fees() # COBOL: PERFORM 8400-DEDUCT-FEES.

def calculate_monthly_fee(account_type: str) -> Decimal:
    """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
    match account_type: # COBOL: EVALUATE WS-ACCOUNT-TYPE
        case 'CHK': # COBOL: WHEN 'CHK'
            monthly_fee = Decimal('12.00') # COBOL: MOVE 12.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case 'SAV': # COBOL: WHEN 'SAV'
            monthly_fee = Decimal('5.00') # COBOL: MOVE 5.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case 'PRM': # COBOL: WHEN 'PRM'
            monthly_fee = Decimal('25.00') # COBOL: MOVE 25.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case _: # COBOL: WHEN OTHER
            monthly_fee = Decimal('0.00') # COBOL: MOVE 0.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
    return monthly_fee # COBOL: END-EVALUATE.

def calculate_transaction_fees(trans_count: int, free_trans_limit: int, per_trans_fee: Decimal) -> tuple[int, Decimal]:
    """8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES."""
    if trans_count > free_trans_limit: # COBOL: IF WS-TRANS-COUNT > WS-FREE-TRANS-LIMIT
        excess_trans = trans_count - free_trans_limit # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-EXCESS-TRANS = WS-TRANS-COUNT - WS-FREE-TRANS-LIMIT
        trans_fee = excess_trans * per_trans_fee # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-TRANS-FEE = WS-EXCESS-TRANS * WS-PER-TRANS-FEE
    else: # COBOL: ELSE
        trans_fee = Decimal('0.00') # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TRANS-FEE
        excess_trans = 0
    return excess_trans, trans_fee # COBOL: END-IF.

def apply_fee_waivers(account_balance: Decimal, min_balance_waiver: Decimal, customer_tier: str, trans_fee: Decimal, monthly_fee: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS."""
    if account_balance >= min_balance_waiver: # COBOL: IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE >= WS-MIN-BALANCE-WAIVER
        monthly_fee = Decimal('0.00') # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
    if customer_tier in ('GOLD', 'PLATINUM'): # COBOL: IF WS-CUSTOMER-TIER = 'GOLD' OR 'PLATINUM'
        trans_fee = trans_fee * Decimal('0.5') # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-TRANS-FEE = WS-TRANS-FEE * 0.5
    return trans_fee, monthly_fee # COBOL: END-IF.

def deduct_fees(monthly_fee: Decimal, trans_fee: Decimal, account_balance: Decimal, update_account, record_fee_transaction) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """8400-DEDUCT-FEES."""
    total_fees = monthly_fee + trans_fee # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-FEES = WS-MONTHLY-FEE + WS-TRANS-FEE
    account_balance -= total_fees # COBOL: SUBTRACT WS-TOTAL-FEES FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account() # COBOL: PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
    record_fee_transaction() # COBOL: PERFORM 8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION.
    return total_fees, account_balance

def record_fee_transaction(Txn_account_id: str, total_fees: Decimal, Fee_record: type, current_date) -> None:
    """8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION."""
    fee_record = Fee_record()
    fee_record.Fee_account = Txn_account_id # COBOL: MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO FEE-ACCOUNT
    fee_record.Fee_amount = total_fees # COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-FEES TO FEE-AMOUNT
    fee_record.Fee_description = 'MONTHLY FEE' # COBOL: MOVE 'MONTHLY FEE' TO FEE-DESCRIPTION
    fee_record.Fee_date = current_date() # COBOL: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO FEE-DATE
    return fee_record # COBOL: WRITE FEE-RECORD FROM WS-FEE-RECORD.

def finalization(write_control_totals, close_files, display_summary):
    """9000-FINALIZATION."""
    write_control_totals() # COBOL: PERFORM 9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS
    close_files() # COBOL: PERFORM 9200-CLOSE-FILES
    display_summary() # COBOL: PERFORM 9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY.

def write_control_totals(trans_count: int, total_deposits: Decimal, total_withdrawals: Decimal, error_count: int, Ctl_record: type, current_date) -> None:
    """9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS."""
    control_record = Ctl_record()
    control_record.Ctl_trans_count = trans_count # COBOL: MOVE WS-TRANS-COUNT TO CTL-TRANS-COUNT
    control_record.Ctl_deposits = total_deposits # COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO CTL-DEPOSITS
    control_record.Ctl_withdrawals = total_withdrawals # COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO CTL-WITHDRAWALS
    control_record.Ctl_error_count = error_count # COBOL: MOVE WS-ERROR-COUNT TO CTL-ERROR-COUNT
    control_record.Ctl_run_date = current_date() # COBOL: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO CTL-RUN-DATE
    return control_record # COBOL: WRITE CONTROL-RECORD FROM WS-CONTROL-RECORD.

def close_files(customer_file, account_file, transaction_file, report_file, error_file, master_file):
    """9200-CLOSE-FILES."""
    customer_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE CUSTOMER-FILE
    account_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE ACCOUNT-FILE
    transaction_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE TRANSACTION-FILE
    report_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE REPORT-FILE
    error_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE ERROR-FILE
    master_file.close() # COBOL: CLOSE MASTER-FILE.

def display_summary(trans_count: int, deposit_count: int, withdrawal_count: int, transfer_count: int, error_count: int, total_deposits: Decimal, total_withdrawals: Decimal, net_change: Decimal):
    """9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY."""
    print('==========================================') # COBOL: DISPLAY '=========================================='
    print('MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE'
    print('==========================================') # COBOL: DISPLAY '=========================================='
    print(f'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED:  {trans_count}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ' WS-TRANS-COUNT
    print(f'DEPOSITS:               {deposit_count}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'DEPOSITS:              ' WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
    print(f'WITHDRAWALS:            {withdrawal_count}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'WITHDRAWALS:           ' WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
    print(f'TRANSFERS:              {transfer_count}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'TRANSFERS:             ' WS-TRANSFER-COUNT
    print(f'ERRORS:                 {error_count}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'ERRORS:                ' WS-ERROR-COUNT
    print(f'TOTAL DEPOSITS:   ${total_deposits}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'TOTAL DEPOSITS:   $' WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
    print(f'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS:$ {total_withdrawals}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS:$' WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
    print(f'NET CHANGE:       $ {net_change}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'NET CHANGE:       $' WS-NET-CHANGE
    print('==========================================') # COBOL: DISPLAY '=========================================='.

def abort_process(abort_reason: str, current_date, close_files):
    """9500-ABORT-PROCESS."""
    print(f'CRITICAL ERROR: {abort_reason}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'CRITICAL ERROR: ' WS-ABORT-REASON
    print(f'PROCESSING ABORTED AT {current_date()}') # COBOL: DISPLAY 'PROCESSING ABORTED AT ' FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
    close_files() # COBOL: PERFORM 9200-CLOSE-FILES
    raise SystemExit(8) # COBOL: STOP RUN WITH STATUS 8.

class LoanProcessing:

    def __init__(self):
        self.WS_LTV_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS: int = 0
        self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS: int = 0
        self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS: int = 0
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE: int = 0
        self.WS_FACTOR_1: str = ""
        self.WS_FACTOR_2: str = ""
        self.WS_FACTOR_3: str = ""
        self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY: str = ""
        self.WS_CREDIT_TIER: str = ""
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS: str = ""
        self.WS_CONDITIONS: str = ""
        self.WS_DTI_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_BASE_RATE: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_APPROVED_RATE: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE: int = 0
        self.WS_AMORT_IDX: int = 0
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH: int = 0
        self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR: int = 0
        self.AMORT_INTEREST: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.AMORT_BALANCE: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM: List[int] = [0] * 360
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.AMORT_ESCROW: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT: List[Decimal] = [Decimal(0)] * 360
        self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE: int = 0

        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS: str = ""

        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE: str = ""

        self.WS_HOLD_IDX: int = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG: str = ""

        self.WS_HOLDING: List[HoldingRec] = []
        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL: str = ""

        self.WS_TRADE_ID: str = ""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG: str = ""
        self.WS_ERROR_MSG: str = ""
        self.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE: int = 0
        self.WS_INSURED_AGE: int = 0
        self.WS_SMOKER_FLAG: str = ""
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal(0)
        self.WS_VEHICLE_AGE: int = 0
        self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS: int = 360

    def calculate_pmi(self) -> None:
        """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
                )
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
                )
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
                )
            case _:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0050") / 12
                )

    def evaluate_history(self) -> None:
        """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = "SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY"
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = "60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES"
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = "MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES"

    def calculate_final_risk(self) -> None:
        """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE // 4
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "LOW RISK"
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "MODERATE"
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "ELEVATED"
            case _:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "HIGH RISK"

    def determine_approval(self) -> None:
        """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == "F":
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW"
            return

        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == "HIGH RISK":
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED"
            return

        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "DTI RATIO TOO HIGH"
            return

        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "A"
        self.calculate_approved_terms()

    def calculate_approved_terms(self) -> None:
        """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case "A":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.00")
            case "B":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.50")
            case "C":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("1.50")
            case "D":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("3.00")
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == "ELEVATED":
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal("0.50")

    def generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (
            self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
            * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
            * self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR
            / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

    def create_amortization(self) -> None:
        """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = 20240101 # Replace with actual current date logic.

        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
        self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR = 2024 # Replace with actual current year logic.

        for self.WS_AMORT_IDX in range(1, self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1):
            self.calculate_payment_split()

    def calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
        amort_idx = self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[amort_idx] = (
            self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        )
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[amort_idx] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - self.AMORT_INTEREST[amort_idx]
        )
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[amort_idx]
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[amort_idx] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[amort_idx] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[amort_idx] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT

        if True:  # Assuming LOAN-MORTGAGE is always true for this example
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[amort_idx] = (
                self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM
            ) / 12
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[amort_idx] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
                + self.AMORT_ESCROW[amort_idx]
                + self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT
            )
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[amort_idx] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT

        self.advance_payment_date()

    def advance_payment_date(self) -> None:
        """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
        amort_idx = self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1

        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[amort_idx] = (
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1
        )

    def finalize_loan(self) -> None:
        """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = 20240101 # Replace with actual current date logic.
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = (
            self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = "A"
        self.create_loan_record()
        self.disburse_funds()
        self.send_confirmation()

    def create_loan_record(self) -> None:
        """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
        loan_record = LoanRecord(
            loan_rec_id=self.WS_LOAN_ID,
            loan_rec_type=self.WS_LOAN_TYPE,
            loan_rec_amount=self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT,
            loan_rec_rate=self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE,
            loan_rec_payment=self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT,
            loan_rec_start=self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE,
            loan_rec_status=self.WS_LOAN_STATUS,
        )
        #WRITE LOAN-RECORD FROM WS-LOAN-RECORD. Placeholder for writing to a file or database.

    def disburse_funds(self) -> None:
        """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.process_deposit()
        self.write_audit_trail()

    def send_confirmation(self) -> None:
        """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = "LOAN-CONFIRM"

        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = "Your loan has been approved"
        self.send_notification()

    def process_decline(self) -> None:
        """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = "DECLINED"
        self.record_decline()
        self.send_decline_notice()

    def record_decline(self) -> None:

        decline_record = DeclineRecord(

        )
        #WRITE DECLINE-RECORD FROM WS-DECLINE-RECORD. Placeholder for writing to a file or database.

    def send_decline_notice(self) -> None:

        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = "LOAN-DECLINE"

    def portfolio_management(self) -> None:
        """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.load_portfolio()
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_values()
        self.rebalance_check()
        self.generate_statements()

    def load_portfolio(self) -> None:
        """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "" #Setting to Empty string to get into loop.
        while not (self.WS_HOLD_IDX > 100 or self.WS_EOF_FLAG == "Y"):
            try:
                holding = HoldingRec("ABC", 100, Decimal("10.00"), Decimal("12.00"), Decimal("1200.00"), Decimal("200.00"), Decimal("16.67"), "STK", 20230101) # Replace with actual data.
                self.WS_HOLDING.append(holding)
                self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
            except EOFError:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "Y"
        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

    def update_market_prices(self) -> None:
        """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_symbol
            self.get_quote()
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_current_price = self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE

    def get_quote(self) -> None:
        """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
        QUOTE_REQUEST_SYMBOL = self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
        QUOTE_REQUEST = QUOTE_REQUEST_SYMBOL
        QUOTE_RESPONSE_STATUS = 'OK' #Simulating Successful call.
        QUOTE_LAST_PRICE = Decimal("15.00")
        QUOTE_RESPONSE = QUOTE_LAST_PRICE
        #CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING QUOTE-REQUEST QUOTE-RESPONSE Placeholder for API call.
        if QUOTE_RESPONSE_STATUS == "OK":
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = QUOTE_LAST_PRICE
        else:
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal("0.00")

    def calculate_values(self) -> None:
        """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal("0.00")
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            self.calculate_holding_value()

    def calculate_holding_value(self) -> None:
        """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
        hold_idx = self.WS_HOLD_IDX -1
        self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_market_value = (
            Decimal(self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_shares) * self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_current_price
        )
        self.WS_HOLD_COST = (
            Decimal(self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_shares)
            * self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_cost_per_share
        )
        self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_gain_loss = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_market_value - self.WS_HOLD_COST
        )
        if self.WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
            self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_pct_change = (
                self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_gain_loss / self.WS_HOLD_COST
            ) * 100
        else:
            self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")

        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_market_value
        self.WS_COST_BASIS += self.WS_HOLD_COST
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_gain_loss

    def rebalance_check(self) -> None:
        """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
        self.calculate_current_allocation()
        self.compare_to_target()
        if self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == "Y":
            self.generate_rebalance_trades()

    def calculate_current_allocation(self) -> None:
        """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
        self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BONDS_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_CASH_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            match self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX-1].hold_type:
                case "STK":
                    self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX-1].hold_market_value
                case "BND":
                    self.WS_BONDS_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX-1].hold_market_value
                case "CSH":
                    self.WS_CASH_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX-1].hold_market_value

        self.WS_STOCKS_PCT = (self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_BONDS_PCT = (self.WS_BONDS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_CASH_PCT = (self.WS_CASH_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100

    def compare_to_target(self) -> None:
        """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
        self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = "N"
        self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF = self.WS_STOCKS_PCT - self.WS_TARGET_STOCKS_PCT
        self.WS_BONDS_DIFF = self.WS_BONDS_PCT - self.WS_TARGET_BONDS_PCT

        if abs(self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF) > 5:
            self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = "Y"
        if abs(self.WS_BONDS_DIFF) > 5:
            self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = "Y"

    def generate_rebalance_trades(self) -> None:
        """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""
        if self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF > 0:
            self.WS_SELL_AMOUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE * self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF / 100
            self.create_sell_order()
        else:
            self.WS_BUY_AMOUNT = (
                self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE * (0 - self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF) / 100
            )
            self.create_buy_order()

    def create_sell_order(self) -> None:
        """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
        self.WS_TRADE_TYPE = "SELL"
        self.WS_ORDER_TYPE = "MARKET"
        self.WS_SELL_AMOUNT = Decimal("100.00") #Hardcoding a value.
        self.WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = self.WS_SELL_AMOUNT
        self.trade_execution()

    def create_buy_order(self) -> None:
        """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
        self.WS_TRADE_TYPE = "BUY "
        self.WS_ORDER_TYPE = "MARKET"
        self.WS_BUY_AMOUNT = Decimal("100.00") #Hardcoding a value.
        self.WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = self.WS_BUY_AMOUNT
        self.trade_execution()

    def generate_statements(self) -> None:
        """11500-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
        self.monthly_statement()
        if self.WS_END_OF_QUARTER == "Y":
            self.quarterly_report()
        if self.WS_END_OF_YEAR == "Y":

        """11510-MONTHLY-STATEMENT."""
        RPT_TITLE = "MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT" #Used only locally, not storing as field.
        self.write_holdings_detail()

    def write_holdings_detail(self) -> None:
        """11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL."""

            hold_idx = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1
            RPT_SYMBOL = self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_symbol #Used only locally, not storing as field.

            holdings_line = HoldingsLine(RPT_SYMBOL, RPT_SHARES, RPT_PRICE, RPT_VALUE, RPT_GAIN)

    def quarterly_report(self) -> None:
        """11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT."""
        RPT_TITLE = "QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT" #Used only locally, not storing as field.
        RPT_QUARTER_RETURN = (

        ) * 100 #Used only locally, not storing as field.
        performance_line = PerformanceLine(RPT_QUARTER_RETURN)

    def trade_execution(self) -> None:
        """12000-TRADE-EXECUTION."""
        self.validate_order()
        if self.WS_ORDER_VALID == "Y":
            self.check_funds_shares()
            if self.WS_SUFFICIENT_FLAG == "Y":
                self.route_order()
                self.execute_order()
                self.settle_trade()
            else:
                self.reject_order()

    PUSH_STATUS: str = ''
    WS_SCREENING_DATE: str = ''
    WS_WATCHLIST_HITS: int = 0
    WS_SANCTIONS_HIT: str = ''
    WS_OFAC_SCORE: str = ''
    WS_CUSTOMER_NAME: str = ''
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME: str = ''
    OFAC_MATCH_FOUND: str = ''
    PEP_SEARCH_NAME: str = ''
    PEP_MATCH_FOUND: str = ''

def calc_auto_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """Lines: 13210-13220"""
    match data.WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            # ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(100)
        case _:
            # ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(50)

    # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
    if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.5')

    # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
    if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        # COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
        data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
        # ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

    # IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
    if data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        # COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
        data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
        # ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def calc_home_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM."""
    # COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.003')
    # EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
    match data.WS_HOME_AGE:
        # WHEN 0 THRU 10
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('0.9')
        # WHEN 11 THRU 25
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.0')
        # WHEN 26 THRU 50
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            # MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.2')
        # WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.5')

    # IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
    if data.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.5')

    # IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
    if data.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('0.9')

    # COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
    data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal(1000) * Decimal(50)
    # SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    # IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
    if data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < Decimal(200):
        # MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(200)
    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def calc_health_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM."""
    # MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(300)
    # EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
    match data.WS_INSURED_AGE:
        # WHEN 0 THRU 18
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            # MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('0.5')
        # WHEN 19 THRU 30
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.0')
        # WHEN 31 THRU 40
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.3')
        # WHEN 41 THRU 50
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.6')
        # WHEN 51 THRU 60
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            # MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('2.0')
        # WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('2.8')

    # EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
    match data.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        # WHEN 'BRONZE'
        case 'BRONZE':
            # MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('0.8')
        # WHEN 'SILVER'
        case 'SILVER':
            # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.0')
        # WHEN 'GOLD'
        case 'GOLD':
            # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.3')
        # WHEN 'PLATINUM'
        case 'PLATINUM':
            # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.6')

    # IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
    if data.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        # MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal('2.5')

    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal(12)

def underwriting(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13300-UNDERWRITING."""
    # PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
    evaluate_risk_factors(data)
    # PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
    check_medical_history(data)
    # PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
    verify_information(data)
    # PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION
    determine_decision(data)

def evaluate_risk_factors(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS."""
    # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    data.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    # IF POLICY-LIFE
    if data.POLICY_LIFE:
        # IF WS-BMI > 30
        if data.WS_BMI > 30:
            # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        # IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
        if data.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            # ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        # IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
        if data.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
    # IF POLICY-AUTO
    if data.POLICY_AUTO:
        # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
        if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
        if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

def check_medical_history(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY."""
    # IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
    if data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        # COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
        data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5)
        # ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += int(data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS)
    # IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
    if data.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
    # IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
    if data.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        # ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

def verify_information(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION."""
    # PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
    check_fraud_indicators(data)
    # PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS
    validate_documents(data)

def check_fraud_indicators(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS."""
    # IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
    if data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
        data.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
    # IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
    if data.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

def validate_documents(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS."""
    # IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
    if data.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        # MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        # MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION."""
    # EVALUATE TRUE
    match True:
        # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
            # MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
            # MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal('1.5')
        # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
            # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        # WHEN OTHER
        case _:
            # MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy(data: Data, generate_policy_number, create_policy_record, set_beneficiaries, send_policy_docs, send_decline_letter) -> None:
    """Line 13400-ISSUE-POLICY."""
    # IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
    if data.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        # PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
        generate_policy_number(data)
        # PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
        create_policy_record(data)
        # PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
        set_beneficiaries(data)
        # PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
        send_policy_docs(data)
    else:
        # PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER
        send_decline_letter(data)

def generate_policy_number(data: Data, current_date, random, string) -> None:
    """Line 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER."""
    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    data.WS_DATE_PART = current_date()
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
    WS_TYPE_PART = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE  # Assuming this is a local variable as it wasn't in the dataclass
    # COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    data.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random() * 99999)
    # STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER
    data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = string(WS_TYPE_PART, data.WS_DATE_PART, str(data.WS_RANDOM_PART))

def create_policy_record(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD."""
    # INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD #Assuming WS_POLICY_RECORD is external to the function
    WS_POLICY_RECORD = {}
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
    POLICY_REC_NUMBER = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
    POLICY_REC_TYPE = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    # MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
    POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
    # MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
    POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    # MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
    POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = data.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE
    # MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
    POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = data.WS_EXPIRATION_DATE
    # MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
    POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
    # WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD
    POLICY_RECORD = WS_POLICY_RECORD #Assign WS_POLICY_RECORD to POLICY_RECORD
    return POLICY_RECORD, POLICY_REC_NUMBER, POLICY_REC_TYPE, POLICY_REC_COVERAGE, POLICY_REC_PREMIUM, POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE, POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE, POLICY_REC_STATUS


def set_beneficiaries(data: Data) -> None:
    """Line 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES."""
    # PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
    for data.WS_BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6):
        # IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
        if data.BENEF_NAME[data.WS_BENEF_IDX] != '':
            # INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC #Assuming WS_BENEFICIARY_REC is external to the function
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC = {}
            # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
            BENEF_REC_POLICY = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            # MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-NAME
            BENEF_REC_NAME = data.BENEF_NAME[data.WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # MOVE BENEF-RELATION(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-RELATION
            BENEF_REC_RELATION = data.BENEF_RELATION[data.WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # MOVE BENEF-PCT(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-PCT
            BENEF_REC_PCT = data.BENEF_PCT[data.WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
            BENEFICIARY_RECORD = WS_BENEFICIARY_REC #Assign WS_BENEFICIARY_REC to BENEFICIARY_RECORD
            return BENEFICIARY_RECORD, BENEF_REC_POLICY, BENEF_REC_NAME, BENEF_REC_RELATION, BENEF_REC_PCT


def send_policy_docs(data: Data, send_notification) -> None:
    """Line 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS."""
    # MOVE 'POLICY-ISSUE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'POLICY-ISSUE'
    # MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    # STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-POLICY-NUMBER DELIMITED SIZE ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Your policy {data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER} has been issued'
    # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION
    send_notification(data)

def send_decline_letter(data: Data, send_notification) -> None:
    """Line 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER."""
    # MOVE 'POLICY-DECLINE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'POLICY-DECLINE'
    # MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    # MOVE 'Regarding your insurance application' TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION
    send_notification(data)

def claims_handling(data: Data, receive_claim, validate_claim, investigate_claim, adjudicate_claim, process_payment) -> None:
    """Line 13500-CLAIMS-HANDLING."""
    # PERFORM 13510-RECEIVE-CLAIM
    receive_claim(data)
    # PERFORM 13520-VALIDATE-CLAIM
    validate_claim(data)
    # PERFORM 13530-INVESTIGATE-CLAIM
    investigate_claim(data)
    # PERFORM 13540-ADJUDICATE-CLAIM
    adjudicate_claim(data)
    # PERFORM 13550-PROCESS-PAYMENT
    process_payment(data)

def receive_claim(data: Data, current_date, generate_claim_number) -> None:
    """Line 13510-RECEIVE-CLAIM."""
    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CLAIM-DATE
    data.WS_CLAIM_DATE = current_date()
    # PERFORM 13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER
    generate_claim_number(data)
    # MOVE 'RECEIVED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS
    data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'RECEIVED'

def generate_claim_number(data: Data, current_date, random, string) -> None:
    """Line 13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER."""
    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    data.WS_DATE_PART = current_date()
    # COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    data.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random() * 99999)
    # STRING 'CLM' DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-CLAIM-NUMBER
    data.WS_CLAIM_NUMBER = string('CLM', data.WS_DATE_PART, str(data.WS_RANDOM_PART))

def validate_claim(data: Data, check_policy_status, check_coverage, check_deductible) -> None:
    """Line 13520-VALIDATE-CLAIM."""
    # PERFORM 13522-CHECK-POLICY-STATUS
    check_policy_status(data)
    # PERFORM 13524-CHECK-COVERAGE
    check_coverage(data)
    # PERFORM 13526-CHECK-DEDU
