    def move_y_to_ws_pep_status(self):
        """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PEP-STATUS"""
        self.WS_PEP_STATUS = 'Y'
        """MOVE PEP-MATCH-SCORE TO WS-PEP-SCORE"""
        self.WS_PEP_SCORE = self.PEP_MATCH_SCORE

    def check_adverse_media(self):
        """16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA."""
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME"""
        media_search_name = self.WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
        """CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE"""
        self.MEDIA_RESPONSE = self.mediasrch(self.MEDIA_REQUEST)
        """IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0"""
        if self.MEDIA_RESPONSE.media_hits_found > 0:
            """ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS"""
            self.WS_WATCHLIST_HITS += self.MEDIA_RESPONSE.media_hits_found

    def calculate_match_score(self):
        """16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE."""
        """IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0"""
        if self.WS_OFAC_SCORE > 0:
            """ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE"""
            self.WS_MATCH_SCORE += self.WS_OFAC_SCORE
        """IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0"""
        if self.WS_PEP_SCORE > 0:
            """ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE"""
            self.WS_MATCH_SCORE += self.WS_PEP_SCORE
        """COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE = WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS."""
        self.WS_MATCH_SCORE = self.WS_MATCH_SCORE / self.WS_WATCHLIST_HITS if self.WS_WATCHLIST_HITS else 0

    def determine_disposition(self):
        """16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION."""
        """EVALUATE TRUE"""
        match True:
            """WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90"""
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 90:
                """MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE"""
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'CONFIRMED'
                """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED"""
                self.WS_SAR_REQUIRED = 'Y'
            """WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75"""
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 75:
                """MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE"""
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'POTENTIAL'
                """MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS"""
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'REVIEW'
            """WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50"""
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 50:
                """MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE"""
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'WEAK'
                """MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS"""
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'
            """WHEN OTHER"""
            case _:
                """MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE"""
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
                """MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS"""
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'

    def kyc_verification(self):
        """16200-KYC-VERIFICATION."""
        """PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY"""
        self.verify_identity()
        """PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS"""
        self.verify_address()
        """PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS"""
        self.verify_documents()
        """PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS."""
        self.determine_kyc_status()

    def verify_identity(self):
        """16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY."""
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN"""
        self.ID_VERIFY_SSN = self.WS_CUSTOMER_SSN
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB"""
        self.ID_VERIFY_DOB = self.WS_CUSTOMER_DOB
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME"""
        self.ID_VERIFY_NAME = self.WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
        """CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE"""
        self.ID_RESPONSE = self.idverify(self.ID_REQUEST)
        """IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'"""
        if self.ID_RESPONSE.id_verified == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS"""
            self.WS_ID_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS"""
            self.WS_ID_STATUS = 'FAILED'

    def verify_address(self):
        """16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS."""
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT"""
        self.ADDR_VERIFY_INPUT = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS
        """CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE"""
        self.ADDR_RESPONSE = self.addrverify(self.ADDR_REQUEST)
        """IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'"""
        if self.ADDR_RESPONSE.addr_verified == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS"""
            self.WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS"""
            self.WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'UNVERIFIED'

    def verify_documents(self):
        """16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS."""
        """IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'"""
        if self.WS_DOC_TYPE == 'PASSPORT':
            """PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT"""
            self.verify_passport()
        """ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'"""
        elif self.WS_DOC_TYPE == 'LICENSE':
            """PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE"""
            self.verify_license()
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC"""
            self.verify_other_doc()

    def verify_passport(self):
        """16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT."""
        """MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM"""
        self.PASSPORT_VERIFY_NUM = self.WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER
        """MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY"""
        self.PASSPORT_VERIFY_COUNTRY = self.WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY
        """CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP"""
        self.PASSPORT_RESP = self.passverify(self.PASSPORT_REQ)
        """IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'"""
        if self.PASSPORT_RESP.passport_valid == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

    def verify_license(self):
        """16234-VERIFY-LICENSE."""
        """MOVE WS-LICENSE-NUMBER TO LICENSE-VERIFY-NUM"""
        self.LICENSE_VERIFY_NUM = self.WS_LICENSE_NUMBER
        """MOVE WS-LICENSE-STATE TO LICENSE-VERIFY-STATE"""
        self.LICENSE_VERIFY_STATE = self.WS_LICENSE_STATE
        """CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP"""
        self.LICENSE_RESP = self.licverify(self.LICENSE_REQ)
        """IF LICENSE-VALID = 'Y'"""
        if self.LICENSE_RESP.license_valid == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

    def verify_other_doc(self):
        """16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC."""
        """MOVE 'MANUAL REVIEW' TO WS-DOC-STATUS."""
        self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'MANUAL REVIEW'

    def determine_kyc_status(self):
        """16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS."""
        """IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'"""
        if self.WS_ID_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and self.WS_ADDR_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and self.WS_DOC_STATUS == 'VERIFIED':
            """MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_KYC_STATUS = 'APPROVED'
        """ELSE"""
        else:
            """MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS"""
            self.WS_KYC_STATUS = 'PENDING'

    def sanctions_check(self):
        """16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK."""
        """IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'"""
        if self.WS_SANCTIONS_HIT == 'Y':
            """PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE"""
            self.escalate_to_compliance()
            """PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT"""
            self.freeze_account()

    def escalate_to_compliance(self):
        """16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE."""
        """INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD"""
        self.WS_ESCALATION_RECORD = EscRecord()
        """MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON"""
        self.ESC_REASON = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER"""
        self.ESC_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        """MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE"""
        self.ESC_DATE = self.current_date()
        """MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY"""
        self.ESC_PRIORITY = 'URGENT'
        """WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD."""
        self.write_escalation_record(self.WS_ESCALATION_RECORD)

    def freeze_account(self):
        """16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT."""
        """MOVE 'F' TO WS-ACCOUNT-STATUS"""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS = 'F'
        """MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON"""
        self.WS_FREEZE_REASON = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
        """REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD."""
        self.rewrite_account_record()

    def transaction_monitoring(self):
        """16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
        """PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY"""
        self.check_velocity()
        """PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS"""
        self.check_patterns()
        """PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK"""
        self.check_high_risk()
        """PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        self.calculate_risk_score()

    def check_velocity(self):
        """16410-CHECK-VELOCITY."""
        """IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD"""
        if self.WS_DAILY_TRANS_COUNT > self.WS_VELOCITY_THRESHOLD:
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG"""
            self.WS_VELOCITY_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20
        """IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD"""
        if self.WS_DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT > self.WS_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD:
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG"""
            self.WS_AMOUNT_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20

    def check_patterns(self):
        """16420-CHECK-PATTERNS."""
        """IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5"""
        if self.WS_ROUND_AMOUNT_COUNT > 5:
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG"""
            self.WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 15
        """IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'"""
        if self.WS_STRUCTURING_DETECTED == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG"""
            self.WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 30

    def check_high_risk(self):
        """16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK."""
        """IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'"""
        if self.WS_HIGH_RISK_COUNTRY == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG"""
            self.WS_LOCATION_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 25
        """IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'"""
        if self.WS_NEW_DEVICE == 'Y':
            """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG"""
            self.WS_DEVICE_FLAG = 'Y'
            """ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE"""
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 10

    def calculate_risk_score(self):
        """16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        """EVALUATE TRUE"""
        match True:
            """WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80"""
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 80:
                """MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION"""
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'BLOCK'
                """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW"""
                self.WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
            """WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60"""
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 60:
                """MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION"""
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'REVIEW'
                """MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW"""
                self.WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
            """WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40"""
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 40:
                """MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION"""
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'MONITOR'
            """WHEN OTHER"""
            case _:
                """MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION"""
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'APPROVE'

    def suspicious_activity_report(self):
        """16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT."""
        """IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'"""
        if self.WS_SAR_REQUIRED == 'Y':
            """PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA"""
            self.gather_sar_data()
            """PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR"""
            self.generate_sar()
            """PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR"""
            self.file_sar()

        """16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA."""
        """MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO

# === CHUNK 10 (lines 6013-6680) ===
def integer_of_date(date: str) -> int:
    """Placeholder for date conversion to integer"""
    return int(date.replace("-",""))

def random() -> float:
  """ Placeholder for random number generation"""
  return 0.5

def mod(x: int, y: int) -> int:
  """ Placeholder for mod calculation"""
  return x % y

def send_notification(notif_type: str, notif_channel: str, notif_subject: str) -> None:
  """ Placeholder for sending notification"""
  print (f"Sending notification of type {notif_type} via {notif_channel} with subject {notif_subject}")

def pinverify(card_number: str, current_pin: str) -> str:
  """ Placeholder for PIN verification. Returns 'MATCH' or 'NOMATCH'"""
  return 'MATCH' if current_pin == '1234' else 'NOMATCH'

def pinencrypt(new_pin: str) -> str:
  """ Placeholder for PIN encryption"""
  return "ENCRYPTED_" + new_pin

@dataclass
class WSRecords:
    """Workspace Records"""
    ws_last_run_date: str = "2024-01-01"
    ws_next_run_date: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = "N"
    ws_total_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_total_trans_count: int = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_active_customers: int = 0
    ws_new_customers: int = 0
    ws_churned_customers: int = 0
    ws_period_start: str = "2024-01-01"
    ws_response_time_total: int = 0
    ws_response_count: int = 0
    ws_avg_response_time: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_process_date: str = "2024-01-01"
    ws_daily_summary: str = ""
    ws_week_number: int = 1
    ws_day_of_week: int = 7
    ws_weekly_summary: str = ""
    ws_curr_month: int = 1
    ws_curr_year: int = 2024
    ws_end_of_month: str = "N"
    ws_monthly_summary: str = ""
    ws_daily_sum_rec: str = ""
    ws_total_assets: Decimal = Decimal(1000000)
    ws_net_income: Decimal = Decimal(50000)
    ws_total_equity: Decimal = Decimal(200000)
    ws_interest_expense: Decimal = Decimal(10000)
    ws_interest_income: Decimal = Decimal(20000)
    ws_earning_assets: Decimal = Decimal(800000)
    ws_roa: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_roe: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_nim: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_error_count: int = 0
    ws_within_sla_count: int = 100
    ws_total_cases: int = 100
    ws_fcr_count: int = 50
    ws_total_calls: int = 100
    ws_error_rate: Decimal = Decimal(0)

    ws_first_call_resolution: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_marketing_spend: Decimal = Decimal(1000)
    ws_avg_revenue_per_customer: Decimal = Decimal(100)

    ws_churn_rate: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_acquisition_cost: Decimal = Decimal(0)

    ws_total_revenue: Decimal = Decimal(100000)
    ws_fraud_score: int = 100
    ws_npl_ratio: Decimal = Decimal(0.05)

    ws_exec_dashboard: str = ""

    ws_csv_header: str = ""
    ws_csv_line: str = ""
    ws_xml_line: str = ""
    ws_json_line: str = ""
    ws_first_record: str = "N"
    ws_json_comma: str = ""
    ws_notif_type: str = ""
    ws_notif_channel: str = ""
    ws_notif_subject: str = ""
    ws_notif_body: str = ""
    ws_account_rec: str = ""
    ws_days_inactive: int = 0
    ws_dormant_years: int = 0
    ws_escheat_years: int = 5
    ws_escheat_amount: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_escheat_record: str = ""
    ws_close_request: str = "N"
    ws_closure_valid: str = "N"
    ws_closure_reject: str = ""
    ws_final_balance: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_archive_record: str = ""
    ws_reactivate_request: str = "N"
    ws_react_valid: str = "N"
    ws_react_reject: str = ""
    ws_days_since_close: int = 60
    ws_card_prefix: str = ""
    ws_card_bin: str = "123456"
    ws_card_seq: int = 0
    ws_card_number_temp: str = ""
    ws_luhn_check: int = 0
    ws_luhn_sum: int = 0
    ws_luhn_idx: int = 0
    ws_luhn_digit: int = 0
    ws_card_number: str = ""
    ws_card_type: str = "DEBIT"
    ws_card_network: str = ""
    ws_daily_limit: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_atm_limit: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    ws_card_record: str = ""
    ws_activation_request: str = "N"
    ws_cardholder_verified: str = "N"
    ws_cvv_input: str = "123"
    ws_card_cvv: str = "123"
    ws_dob_input: str = "1990-01-01"
    ws_cardholder_dob: str = "1990-01-01"

    ws_cardholder_ssn_last4: str = "1234"
    ws_activation_attempts: int = 0
    ws_pin_change_request: str = "N"

    ws_new_pin: str = "5678"
    ws_pin_verify_result: str = ""
    ws_pin_attempts: int = 0
    ws_encrypted_pin: str = ""
    ws_replace_request: str = "N"
    ws_shipment_record: str = ""
    ws_cardholder_address: str = "123 Main St"
    ws_expedite: str = "N"
    ws_bin_number: str = "555555"

@dataclass
class PerformanceRecord:
    """Performance Record"""
    perf_response_time: int

class CobolToPython:

    def __init__(self):
        self.ws_records: WSRecords = WSRecords()
        self.daily_summary_file = [DailySummaryRecord("2024-01-01", 10, Decimal(100.00), Decimal(50.00), Decimal(50.00), 1)] * 5
        self.transaction_file = [TransactionRecord(Decimal(100.00))] * 5
        self.customer_file = [CustomerRecord("A", "2024-01-01", "2024-01-15")] * 5
        self.perf_log_file = [PerformanceRecord(100)] * 5
        self.account_file = [AccountRecord("2023-01-01", "A", "ACTIVE", "", "12345", Decimal(1000.00), 0, " ", "", "", "John Doe", "123 Main St")] * 5

    def evaluate_next_run_date(self, schedule: str) -> None:
           """
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
              WHEN 'WEEKLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
              WHEN 'MONTHLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
              WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
              WHEN 'YEARLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
           END-EVALUATE.
           """
           match schedule:
                case "DAILY":
                    self.ws_records.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(self.ws_records.ws_last_run_date) + 1
                case "WEEKLY":
                    self.ws_records.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(self.ws_records.ws_last_run_date) + 7
                case "MONTHLY":
                    self.ws_records.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(self.ws_records.ws_last_run_date) + 30
                case "QUARTERLY":
                    self.ws_records.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(self.ws_records.ws_last_run_date) + 90
                case "YEARLY":
                    self.ws_records.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(self.ws_records.ws_last_run_date) + 365

    def data_analytics(self) -> None:
        """21000-DATA-ANALYTICS.
           PERFORM 21100-COLLECT-METRICS
           PERFORM 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA
           PERFORM 21300-CALCULATE-KPI
           PERFORM 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD
           PERFORM 21500-EXPORT-DATA."""
        self.collect_metrics() # 21100-COLLECT-METRICS
        self.aggregate_data() # 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA
        self.calculate_kpi() # 21300-CALCULATE-KPI
        self.generate_dashboard() # 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD
        self.export_data() # 21500-EXPORT-DATA

    def collect_metrics(self) -> None:
        """21100-COLLECT-METRICS.
           PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
           PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
           PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS."""
        self.collect_transaction_metrics() # 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
        self.collect_customer_metrics() # 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
        self.collect_performance_metrics() # 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS

    def collect_transaction_metrics(self) -> None:
        """21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
                    ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT = 
                 WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
           END-IF
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
        self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal(0)
        self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal(0)
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        for trans_rec in self.transaction_file:
            self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count += 1
            self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_amount += trans_rec.trans_amount
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        if self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_avg_trans_amount = self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_amount / self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    def collect_customer_metrics(self) -> None:
        """21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                       ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
                    IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                       ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
                    IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                       ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
        self.ws_records.ws_active_customers = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_new_customers = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_churned_customers = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        for cust_rec in self.customer_file:
            if cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                self.ws_records.ws_active_customers += 1
            if cust_rec.cust_open_date >= self.ws_records.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_records.ws_new_customers += 1
            if cust_rec.cust_close_date >= self.ws_records.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_records.ws_churned_customers += 1
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    def collect_performance_metrics(self) -> None:
        """21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
                    ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME = 
                 WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           END-IF
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
        self.ws_records.ws_response_time_total = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_response_count = 0
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        for perf_rec in self.perf_log_file:
            self.ws_records.ws_response_time_total += perf_rec.perf_response_time
            self.ws_records.ws_response_count += 1
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        if self.ws_records.ws_response_count > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_avg_response_time = Decimal(self.ws_records.ws_response_time_total / self.ws_records.ws_response_count)
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    def aggregate_data(self) -> None:
        """21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.
           PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
           PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
           PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION."""
        self.daily_aggregation() # 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
        self.weekly_aggregation() # 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
        self.monthly_aggregation() # 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION

    def daily_aggregation(self) -> None:
        """21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
           INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
           MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
           WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY."""
        daily_summary = DailySummaryRecord(
            daily_date=self.ws_records.ws_process_date,
            daily_trans_count=self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count,
            daily_trans_amount=self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_amount,
            daily_deposits=Decimal(50.00),
            daily_withdrawals=Decimal(50.00),
            daily_month = self.ws_records.ws_curr_month
        )
        # Assuming a file write operation here.

    def weekly_aggregation(self) -> None:
        """21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.
           IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
              INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
              MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
              PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
              WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
           END-IF."""
        if self.ws_records.ws_day_of_week == 7:
            weekly_summary = WeeklySummaryRecord(
                weekly_week=self.ws_records.ws_week_number,
                weekly_trans_count=0,
                weekly_trans_amount=Decimal(0)
            )
            weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count, weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = self.sum_week_data()
            # Assuming a file write operation here.

    def sum_week_data(self) -> tuple[int, Decimal]:
        """21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           PERFORM 7 TIMES
              ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
              ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           END-PERFORM."""
        weekly_trans_count = 0
        weekly_trans_amount = Decimal(0)
        for _ in range(7):
            # Assuming access to the last 7 days of daily_summary records
            weekly_trans_count += self.daily_summary_file[-1].daily_trans_count
            weekly_trans_amount += self.daily_summary_file[-1].daily_trans_amount
        return weekly_trans_count, weekly_trans_amount

    def monthly_aggregation(self) -> None:
        """21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
           IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
              INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
              MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
              MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
              PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
              WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
           END-IF."""
        if self.ws_records.ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
            monthly_summary = MonthlySummaryRecord(
                monthly_month=self.ws_records.ws_curr_month,
                monthly_year=self.ws_records.ws_curr_year,
                monthly_trans_count=0,
                monthly_trans_amount=Decimal(0),
                monthly_new_accounts=0,
                monthly_closed_accounts=0
            )
            monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count, monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = self.sum_month_data()
            # Assuming a file write operation here.

    def sum_month_data(self) -> tuple[int, Decimal]:
        """21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                       ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                       ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
        monthly_trans_count = 0
        monthly_trans_amount = Decimal(0)
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        for daily_sum_rec in self.daily_summary_file:
            if daily_sum_rec.daily_month == self.ws_records.ws_curr_month:
                monthly_trans_count += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                monthly_trans_amount += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        self.ws_records.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        return monthly_trans_count, monthly_trans_amount

    def calculate_kpi(self) -> None:
        """21300-CALCULATE-KPI.
           PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
           PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
           PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI."""
        self.calc_financial_kpi() # 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
        self.calc_operational_kpi() # 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
        self.calc_customer_kpi() # 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI

    def calc_financial_kpi(self) -> None:
        """21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.
           IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ROA = 
                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF
           IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ROE = 
                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
           END-IF
           IF WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE > 0
              COMPUTE WS-NIM = 
                 ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) /
                  WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF."""
        if self.ws_records.ws_total_assets > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_roa = (self.ws_records.ws_net_income / self.ws_records.ws_total_assets) * 100
        if self.ws_records.ws_total_equity > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_roe = (self.ws_records.ws_net_income / self.ws_records.ws_total_equity) * 100
        if self.ws_records.ws_interest_expense > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_nim = ((self.ws_records.ws_interest_income - self.ws_records.ws_interest_expense) / self.ws_records.ws_earning_assets) * 100

    def calc_operational_kpi(self) -> None:
        """21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI.
           IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE = 
                 (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
           END-IF
           COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE = 
              (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
           COMPUTE WS-FIRST-CALL-RESOLUTION = 
              (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100."""
        if self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_error_rate = Decimal((self.ws_records.ws_error_count / self.ws_records.ws_total_trans_count) * 100)
        self.ws_records.ws_sla_compliance = Decimal((self.ws_records.ws_within_sla_count / self.ws_records.ws_total_cases) * 100)
        self.ws_records.ws_first_call_resolution = Decimal((self.ws_records.ws_fcr_count / self.ws_records.ws_total_calls) * 100)

    def calc_customer_kpi(self) -> None:
        """21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.
           IF WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-CHURN-RATE = 
                 (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
           END-IF
           COMPUTE WS-ACQUISITION-COST = 
              WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
           COMPUTE WS-LIFETIME-VALUE = 
              WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE."""
        if self.ws_records.ws_active_customers > 0:
            self.ws_records.ws_churn_rate = Decimal((self.ws_records.ws_churned_customers / self.ws_records.ws_active_customers) * 100)
        self.ws_records.ws_acquisition_cost = Decimal(self.ws_records.ws_marketing_spend / self.ws_records.ws_new_customers)
        self.ws_records.ws_lifetime_value = Decimal(self.ws_records.ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * self.ws_records.ws_avg_customer_tenure)

    def generate_dashboard(self) -> None:
        """21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD.
           PERFORM 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD
           PERFORM 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD
           PERFORM 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD."""
        self.create_executive_dashboard() # 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD
        self.create_operations_dashboard() # 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD
        self.create_risk_dashboard() # 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD

    def create_executive_dashboard(self) -> None:
        """21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD.
           MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-REVENUE TO DASH-REVENUE
           MOVE WS-NET-INCOME TO DASH-NET-INCOME
           MOVE WS-ROA TO DASH-ROA
           MOVE WS-ROE TO DASH-ROE
           MOVE WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS TO DASH-CUSTOMERS
           WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-EXEC-DASHBOARD."""
        exec_dashboard = DashboardRecord(
            dash_title='EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD',
            dash_revenue=self.ws_records.ws_total_revenue,
            dash_net_income=self.ws_records.ws_net_income,
            dash_roa=self.ws_records.ws_roa,
            dash_roe=self.ws_records.ws_roe,

    def main(self):
      """
      COBOL Line: N/A
      """
      if True:
          self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
          self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2 # COBOL Line: COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
      else:
          self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
          self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7 # COBOL Line: COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
      #self.write_shipment_record(self.WS_SHIPMENT_RECORD) # COBOL Line: WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.
      self.card_blocking() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 23500-CARD-BLOCKING.
      self.wire_transfer() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24000-WIRE-TRANSFER.
      self.ach_processing() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 25000-ACH-PROCESSING.
      self.statement_generation() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 26000-STATEMENT-GENERATION.
      self.overdraft_protection() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 27000-OVERDRAFT-PROTECTION.
      self.interest_accrual() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 28000-INTEREST-ACCRUAL.
      #self.stop_payment() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 29000-STOP-PAYMENT.

    def card_blocking(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 23500-CARD-BLOCKING.
        """
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = self.WS_BLOCK_REASON # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
        #self.rewrite_card_record(self.WS_CARD_RECORD) # COBOL Line: REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {self.WS_BLOCK_REASON}' # COBOL Line: STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
        self.send_notification() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    def wire_transfer(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24000-WIRE-TRANSFER.
        """
        self.validate_wire_request() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
            self.ofac_screening() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
                self.process_wire() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                self.send_confirmation() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
            else:
                self.reject_wire() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE

    def validate_wire_request(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == ' ':
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED

    def ofac_screening(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24200-OFAC-SCREENING.
        """
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
        #self.call_ofacsrch(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE) # COBOL Line: CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
        #self.call_ofacsrch(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE) # COBOL Line: CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT

    def process_wire(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24300-PROCESS-WIRE.
        """
        self.debit_originator() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
        self.create_wire_message() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
        self.transmit_wire() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
        self.record_wire() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE

    def debit_originator(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL Line: SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_FEE # COBOL Line: SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.update_account() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.

    def create_wire_message(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE.
        """
        self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = SwiftMessage() # COBOL Line: INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
        self.SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
        self.SWIFT_TXN_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
        self.SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = self.WS_WIRE_DATE # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
        self.SWIFT_CURRENCY = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
        self.SWIFT_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
        self.SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = self.WS_PURPOSE # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO

    def transmit_wire(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE.
        """
        #self.call_swift_send(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE) # COBOL Line: CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        else:
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            self.reverse_debit() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT

    def record_wire(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24340-RECORD-WIRE.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD = WsWireRecord() # COBOL Line: INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
        self.WIRE_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
        self.WIRE_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
        self.WIRE_STATUS = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
        self.WIRE_FROM_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
        self.WIRE_TO_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
        self.WIRE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
        #self.write_wire_record(self.WS_WIRE_RECORD) # COBOL Line: WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD.

    def reverse_debit(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL Line: ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_FEE # COBOL Line: ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.update_account() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.

    def send_confirmation(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION.
        """
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {self.WS_WIRE_REF} completed' # COBOL Line: STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        self.send_notification() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    def reject_wire(self):
        """
        COBOL Line: 24500-REJECT-WIRE.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC = WsWireRejectRec() # COBOL Line: INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        self.REJECT_WIRE_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
        self.REJECT_REASON = self.WS_WIRE_REJECT # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
        self.REJECT_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL Line: MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
        #self.write_wire_reject_record(self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC) # COBOL Line: WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED' # COBOL Line: MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.send_notification() # COBOL Line: PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    def validate_stop_request(self):
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self):
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.STOP_ACCOUNT = self.ACCT_ID
        self.STOP_CHECK_NUMBER = str(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER)
        self.STOP_AMOUNT = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.STOP_PAYEE = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.STOP_EXPIRY_DATE = int(self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE)) + 180
        self.STOP_STATUS = 'A'
        self.STOP_RECORD = self.WS_STOP_RECORD  # Simplified write

    def apply_stop_fee(self):
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check #{self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification()

    def safe_deposit_box(self):
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()
        self.box_access()
        self.box_drilling()
        self.box_billing()

    def box_rental(self):
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability()
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box()
                self.create_rental_agreement()

    def check_availability(self):
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
                if self.BOX_SIZE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                    break
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def assign_box(self):
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self):
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.RENTAL_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.RENTAL_START_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE]
        self.RENTAL_RECORD = self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT # Simplified Write

    def box_access(self):
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter()
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access()
                self.escort_to_vault()

    def verify_renter(self):
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self):
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.ACCESS_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.ACCESS_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.ACCESS_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")  #CURRENT-TIME
        self.ACCESS_TYPE = 'ENTRY'
        self.ACCESS_LOG_RECORD = self.WS_ACCESS_LOG

    def escort_to_vault(self):
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        print(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG) # display

    def box_drilling(self):
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth()
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling()
                self.notify_renter()

    def validate_drilling_auth(self):
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self):
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.DRILL_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.DRILL_REASON = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE = int(self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE)) + 30
        self.DRILLING_RECORD = self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD

    def notify_renter(self):
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification()

    def box_billing(self):
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                    self.charge_annual_fee()
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self):
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account()
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000

    def merchant_services(self):
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()
        self.capture_transaction()
        self.process_settlement()
        self.handle_chargeback()

    def process_authorization(self):
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score()
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit()
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth()
                else:
                    self.decline_auth()
            else:
                self.decline_auth()
        else:
            self.decline_auth()

    def validate_card(self):
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn()
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry()
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv()
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self):
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1
        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self):
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self):
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        # Assuming 'CVVVERIFY' is an external function
        self.WS_CVV_RESULT = self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV)
        if self.WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        # Assuming 'FRAUDCHECK' is an external function
        self.WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE = self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST)
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self):
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        # Assuming read_card_account_file is implemented
        self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = self.read_card_account_file()
        if self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self):
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code()
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization()

    def generate_auth_code(self):
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(self.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self):
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecord()
        self.AUTH_REC_CARD = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.AUTH_REC_AMOUNT = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.AUTH_REC_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.AUTH_REC_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.AUTH_REC_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S") #CURRENT-TIME
        self.AUTH_REC_MERCHANT = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'P'
        self.AUTH_RECORD = self.WS_AUTH_RECORD

    def decline_auth(self):
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.DECLINE_REC_CARD = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.DECLINE_REC_AMOUNT = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.DECLINE_REC_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.DECLINE_REC_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.DECLINE_RECORD = self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD

    def capture_transaction(self):
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""
        if self.WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_auth_code()
            if self.WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
                self.create_capture_record()

    def validate_auth_code(self):
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        # Assuming read_auth_file and AUTH_FILE are correctly defined
        auth_record = self.read_auth_file() #Uses AUTH_SEARCH_KEY
        if auth_record is None: #INVALID KEY
            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        else: #NOT INVALID KEY
            self.WS_AUTH_REC = auth_record
            if self.AUTH_REC_STATUS == 'P':

# === CHUNK 13 (lines 8017-8684) ===
#        MOVE WS-FILE-RESULT TO FILE-ERR-MSG
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO FILE-ERR-TIMESTAMP
    #        WRITE FILE-ERROR-RECORD FROM WS-FILE-ERROR-LOG.

def write_file_error(file_err_msg: str, file_err_timestamp: date, ws_file_error_log: "WsFileErrorLog") -> None:
    """
    Moves file_err_msg to FILE-ERR-MSG, current date to FILE-ERR-TIMESTAMP, and writes FILE-ERROR-RECORD from WS-FILE-ERROR-LOG.
    """
    file_error_record = ws_file_error_log  # Assuming direct assignment is equivalent to WRITE
    file_error_record.file_err_msg = file_err_msg
    file_error_record.file_err_timestamp = file_err_timestamp
    # Assuming a file write operation here - no actual file I/O in this translation
    print(f"Writing error to file: {file_error_record}")

    #    99800-LOGGING-UTILITIES.
    #        PERFORM 99810-LOG-INFO
    #        PERFORM 99820-LOG-WARNING
    #        PERFORM 99830-LOG-ERROR.

def logging_utilities(log_message: str, ws_log_entry: "WsLogEntry", log_record: "LogRecord") -> None:
    """
    Performs LOG-INFO, LOG-WARNING, and LOG-ERROR.
    """
    log_info(log_message, ws_log_entry, log_record)
    log_warning(log_message, ws_log_entry, log_record)
    log_error(log_message, ws_log_entry, log_record)

    #    99810-LOG-INFO.
    #        MOVE 'INFO' TO LOG-LEVEL
    #        MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    #        WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

def log_info(log_message: str, ws_log_entry: "WsLogEntry", log_record: "LogRecord") -> None:
    """
    Moves 'INFO' to LOG-LEVEL, WS-LOG-MESSAGE to LOG-MESSAGE, current date to LOG-TIMESTAMP, and writes LOG-RECORD from WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    """
    ws_log_entry.log_level = 'INFO'
    ws_log_entry.log_message = log_message
    ws_log_entry.log_timestamp = date.today()
    log_record = ws_log_entry # Assuming a direct assignment is equivalent to WRITE
    # Assuming a file write operation here - no actual file I/O in this translation
    print(f"Writing info log: {log_record}")

    #    99820-LOG-WARNING.
    #        MOVE 'WARN' TO LOG-LEVEL
    #        MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    #        WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

def log_warning(log_message: str, ws_log_entry: "WsLogEntry", log_record: "LogRecord") -> None:
    """
    Moves 'WARN' to LOG-LEVEL, WS-LOG-MESSAGE to LOG-MESSAGE, current date to LOG-TIMESTAMP, and writes LOG-RECORD from WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    """
    ws_log_entry.log_level = 'WARN'
    ws_log_entry.log_message = log_message
    ws_log_entry.log_timestamp = date.today()
    log_record = ws_log_entry # Assuming a direct assignment is equivalent to WRITE
    # Assuming a file write operation here - no actual file I/O in this translation
    print(f"Writing warning log: {log_record}")

    #    99830-LOG-ERROR.
    #        MOVE 'ERROR' TO LOG-LEVEL
    #        MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    #        WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

def log_error(log_message: str, ws_log_entry: "WsLogEntry", log_record: "LogRecord") -> None:
    """
    Moves 'ERROR' to LOG-LEVEL, WS-LOG-MESSAGE to LOG-MESSAGE, current date to LOG-TIMESTAMP, and writes LOG-RECORD from WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    """
    ws_log_entry.log_level = 'ERROR'
    ws_log_entry.log_message = log_message
    ws_log_entry.log_timestamp = date.today()
    log_record = ws_log_entry # Assuming a direct assignment is equivalent to WRITE
    # Assuming a file write operation here - no actual file I/O in this translation
    print(f"Writing error log: {log_record}")

    #    99900-ERROR-HANDLING.
    #        PERFORM 99910-FORMAT-ERROR
    #        PERFORM 99920-DISPLAY-ERROR
    #        PERFORM 99930-WRITE-ERROR-LOG.

def error_handling(ws_error_code: str, ws_error_msg: str, ws_program_name: str, ws_paragraph_name: str, ws_error_log_rec: "WsErrorLogRec", error_log_record: "ErrorLogRecord", ws_formatted_error: str) -> None:
    """
    Performs FORMAT-ERROR, DISPLAY-ERROR, and WRITE-ERROR-LOG.
    """
    formatted_error_string = format_error(ws_error_code, ws_error_msg)
    display_error(formatted_error_string)
    write_error_log(ws_error_code, ws_error_msg, ws_program_name, ws_paragraph_name, ws_error_log_rec, error_log_record)

    #    99910-FORMAT-ERROR.
    #        STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE
    #               WS-ERROR-CODE DELIMITED SIZE
    #               ' - ' DELIMITED SIZE
    #               WS-ERROR-MSG DELIMITED SIZE
    #           INTO WS-FORMATTED-ERROR.

def format_error(ws_error_code: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> str:
    """
    Formats an error message string.
    """
    return f"ERROR: {ws_error_code} - {ws_error_msg}"

    #    99920-DISPLAY-ERROR.
    #        DISPLAY WS-FORMATTED-ERROR.

def display_error(ws_formatted_error: str) -> None:
    """
    Displays the formatted error message.
    """
    print(ws_formatted_error)

    #    99930-WRITE-ERROR-LOG.
    #        INITIALIZE WS-ERROR-LOG-REC
    #        MOVE WS-ERROR-CODE TO ERR-LOG-CODE
    #        MOVE WS-ERROR-MSG TO ERR-LOG-MSG
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ERR-LOG-TIMESTAMP
    #        MOVE WS-PROGRAM-NAME TO ERR-LOG-PROGRAM
    #        MOVE WS-PARAGRAPH-NAME TO ERR-LOG-PARAGRAPH
    #        WRITE ERROR-LOG-RECORD FROM WS-ERROR-LOG-REC.

def write_error_log(ws_error_code: str, ws_error_msg: str, ws_program_name: str, ws_paragraph_name: str, ws_error_log_rec: "WsErrorLogRec", error_log_record: "ErrorLogRecord") -> None:
    """
    Initializes WS-ERROR-LOG-REC, moves error information, and writes ERROR-LOG-RECORD from WS-ERROR-LOG-REC.
    """
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_code = ws_error_code
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_msg = ws_error_msg
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_timestamp = date.today()
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_program = ws_program_name
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_paragraph = ws_paragraph_name
    error_log_record = ws_error_log_rec  # Assuming direct assignment is equivalent to WRITE
    # Assuming a file write operation here - no actual file I/O in this translation
    print(f"Writing to error log: {error_log_record}")

    #   32000-TREASURY-MANAGEMENT.
    #       PERFORM 32100-CALCULATE-CASH-POSITION
    #       PERFORM 32200-PROJECT-CASH-FLOWS
    #       PERFORM 32300-MANAGE-RESERVES
    #       PERFORM 32400-MANAGE-INVESTMENTS
    #       PERFORM 32500-MANAGE-BORROWINGS.

def treasury_management(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_vault_rec: "WsVaultRec", ws_fed_balance: Decimal, ws_corr_rec: "WsCorrRec", ws_loan_pmt_rec: "WsLoanPmtRec", ws_projection_date: date, ws_avg_daily_deposits: Decimal, ws_projection_days: int, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals: Decimal, ws_inv_rec: "WsInvRec", ws_process_date: date, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_reserve_ratio: Decimal, ws_fed_funds_transaction: "WsFedFundsTransaction", ws_fed_funds_rate: Decimal, ws_min_invest_amount: Decimal, investment_data: "InvestmentData", ws_rate_outlook: str, ws_cusip_lookup: str, ws_market_price: Decimal, ws_fhlb_capacity: Decimal, ws_repo_capacity: Decimal, ws_credit_line_avail: Decimal, ws_total_int_expense: Decimal, ws_wholesale_rate: Decimal, ws_borrow_rec: "WsBorrowRec", liquidity_data: "WsLiquidityManagement", ws_internal_limit: Decimal, ws_alert_type: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_stress_level: str, ws_deposit_runoff: Decimal, ws_fed_discount_window: Decimal, ws_asset_sale_capacity: Decimal, ws_cfp_status: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    """32000-TREASURY-MANAGEMENT."""
    calculate_cash_position(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_vault_rec, ws_fed_balance, ws_corr_rec)
    project_cash_flows(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_loan_pmt_rec, ws_projection_date, ws_avg_daily_deposits, ws_projection_days, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals, ws_inv_rec)
    manage_reserves(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_process_date, ws_total_deposits, ws_reserve_ratio, ws_fed_funds_transaction, ws_fed_funds_rate, ws_min_invest_amount)
    manage_investments(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, investment_data, ws_rate_outlook, ws_inv_rec, ws_cusip_lookup, ws_market_price)
    manage_borrowings(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_fhlb_capacity, ws_repo_capacity, ws_credit_line_avail, ws_total_int_expense, ws_wholesale_rate, ws_borrow_rec, ws_process_date)

    #   32100-CALCULATE-CASH-POSITION.
    #       MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CASH-POSITION
    #       PERFORM 32110-SUM-VAULT-CASH
    #       PERFORM 32120-SUM-FED-ACCOUNT
    #       PERFORM 32130-SUM-CORRESPONDENT-BALANCES.

def calculate_cash_position(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_vault_rec: "WsVaultRec", ws_fed_balance: Decimal, ws_corr_rec: "WsCorrRec") -> None:
    """32100-CALCULATE-CASH-POSITION."""
    treasury_data.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_vault_rec)
    sum_fed_account(treasury_data, ws_fed_balance)
    sum_correspondent_balances(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_corr_rec)

    #   32110-SUM-VAULT-CASH.
    #       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    #          READ VAULT-CASH-FILE INTO WS-VAULT-REC
    #             AT END
    #                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    #             NOT AT END
    #                ADD VAULT-BALANCE TO WS-CASH-POSITION
    #          END-READ
    #       END-PERFORM
    #       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

def sum_vault_cash(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_vault_rec: "WsVaultRec") -> None:
    """32110-SUM-VAULT-CASH."""
    # Assume vault_cash_file is a list of WsVaultRec objects
    vault_cash_file = [] # Replace with actual data source
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for ws_vault_rec in vault_cash_file:
        # Simulate READ operation.
        treasury_data.ws_cash_position += ws_vault_rec.vault_balance
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    #   32120-SUM-FED-ACCOUNT.
    #       READ FED-ACCOUNT-FILE INTO WS-FED-BALANCE
    #       ADD WS-FED-BALANCE TO WS-CASH-POSITION.

def sum_fed_account(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_fed_balance: Decimal) -> None:
    """32120-SUM-FED-ACCOUNT."""
    # Assume fed_account_file is a source for Decimal
    fed_account_file = Decimal("100.00") # Replace with actual data source
    treasury_data.ws_cash_position += fed_account_file

    #   32130-SUM-CORRESPONDENT-BALANCES.
    #       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    #          READ CORRESPONDENT-FILE INTO WS-CORR-REC
    #             AT END
    #                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    #             NOT AT END
    #                ADD CORR-BALANCE TO WS-CASH-POSITION
    #          END-READ
    #       END-PERFORM
    #       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

def sum_correspondent_balances(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_corr_rec: "WsCorrRec") -> None:
    """32130-SUM-CORRESPONDENT-BALANCES."""
    # Assume correspondent_file is a list of WsCorrRec objects
    correspondent_file = [] # Replace with actual data source
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for ws_corr_rec in correspondent_file:
        # Simulate READ operation.
        treasury_data.ws_cash_position += ws_corr_rec.corr_balance
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    #   32200-PROJECT-CASH-FLOWS.
    #       MOVE ZEROES TO WS-PROJECTED-INFLOWS
    #       MOVE ZEROES TO WS-PROJECTED-OUTFLOWS
    #       PERFORM 32210-PROJECT-LOAN-PAYMENTS
    #       PERFORM 32220-PROJECT-DEPOSIT-FLOWS
    #       PERFORM 32230-PROJECT-INVESTMENT-MATURITIES
    #       COMPUTE WS-NET-POSITION =
    #          WS-CASH-POSITION + WS-PROJECTED-INFLOWS -
    #          WS-PROJECTED-OUTFLOWS.

def project_cash_flows(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_loan_pmt_rec: "WsLoanPmtRec", ws_projection_date: date, ws_avg_daily_deposits: Decimal, ws_projection_days: int, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals: Decimal, ws_inv_rec: "WsInvRec") -> None:
    """32200-PROJECT-CASH-FLOWS."""
    treasury_data.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    treasury_data.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_loan_pmt_rec, ws_projection_date)
    project_deposit_flows(treasury_data, ws_avg_daily_deposits, ws_projection_days, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals, treasury_data)
    project_investment_maturities(treasury_data, ws_eof_flag, ws_inv_rec, ws_projection_date)
    treasury_data.ws_net_position = treasury_data.ws_cash_position + treasury_data.ws_projected_inflows - treasury_data.ws_projected_outflows

    #   32210-PROJECT-LOAN-PAYMENTS.
    #       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    #          READ LOAN-SCHEDULE-FILE INTO WS-LOAN-PMT-REC
    #             AT END
    #                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    #             NOT AT END
    #                IF LOAN-PMT-DATE <= WS-PROJECTION-DATE
    #                   ADD LOAN-PMT-AMOUNT TO WS-PROJECTED-INFLOWS
    #                END-IF
    #          END-READ
    #       END-PERFORM
    #       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

def project_loan_payments(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_loan_pmt_rec: "WsLoanPmtRec", ws_projection_date: date) -> None:
    """32210-PROJECT-LOAN-PAYMENTS."""
    # Assume loan_schedule_file is a list of WsLoanPmtRec objects
    loan_schedule_file = [] # Replace with actual data source
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for ws_loan_pmt_rec in loan_schedule_file:
        # Simulate READ operation.
        if ws_loan_pmt_rec.loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date:
            treasury_data.ws_projected_inflows += ws_loan_pmt_rec.loan_pmt_amount
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    #   32220-PROJECT-DEPOSIT-FLOWS.
    #       COMPUTE WS-EXPECTED-DEPOSITS =
    #          WS-AVG-DAILY-DEPOSITS * WS-PROJECTION-DAYS
    #       COMPUTE WS-EXPECTED-WITHDRAWALS =
    #          WS-AVG-DAILY-WITHDRAWALS * WS-PROJECTION-DAYS
    #       ADD WS-EXPECTED-DEPOSITS TO WS-PROJECTED-INFLOWS
    #       ADD WS-EXPECTED-WITHDRAWALS TO WS-PROJECTED-OUTFLOWS.

def project_deposit_flows(ws_avg_daily_deposits: Decimal, ws_projection_days: int, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals: Decimal, treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement) -> None:
    """32220-PROJECT-DEPOSIT-FLOWS."""
    expected_deposits = ws_avg_daily_deposits * ws_projection_days
    expected_withdrawals = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals * ws_projection_days
    treasury_data.ws_projected_inflows += expected_deposits
    treasury_data.ws_projected_outflows += expected_withdrawals

    #   32230-PROJECT-INVESTMENT-MATURITIES.
    #       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    #          READ INVESTMENT-FILE INTO WS-INV-REC
    #             AT END
    #                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    #             NOT AT END
    #                IF INV-MATURITY-DATE <= WS-PROJECTION-DATE
    #                   ADD INV-PAR-VALUE TO WS-PROJECTED-INFLOWS
    #                END-IF
    #          END-READ
    #       END-PERFORM
    #       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

def project_investment_maturities(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_inv_rec: "WsInvRec", ws_projection_date: date) -> None:
    """32230-PROJECT-INVESTMENT-MATURITIES."""
    # Assume investment_file is a list of WsInvRec objects
    investment_file = [] # Replace with actual data source
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for ws_inv_rec in investment_file:
        # Simulate READ operation.
        if ws_inv_rec.inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
            treasury_data.ws_projected_inflows += ws_inv_rec.inv_par_value
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    #   32300-MANAGE-RESERVES.
    #       PERFORM 32310-CALCULATE-RESERVE-REQUIREMENT
    #       PERFORM 32320-CHECK-RESERVE-POSITION
    #       IF WS-RESERVE-DEFICIENCY = 'Y'
    #          PERFORM 32330-COVER-RESERVE-SHORTFALL
    #       ELSE
    #          PERFORM 32340-INVEST-EXCESS-RESERVES
    #       END-IF.

def manage_reserves(treasury_data: WsTreasuryManagement, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_process_date: date, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_reserve_ratio: Decimal, ws_fed_funds_transaction: "WsFedFundsTransaction", ws_fed_funds_rate: Decimal, ws_min_invest_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """32300-MANAGE-RESERVES."""
    calculate_reserve_requirement(treasury_data, ws_total_deposits, ws_reserve_ratio)
    reserve_deficiency = check_reserve_position(treasury_data)
    if reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall(treasury_data, ws_process_date, ws_fed_funds_transaction, ws_fed_funds_rate)
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves(treasury_data, ws_min_invest_amount, ws_process_date, ws_fed_funds_transaction, ws_fed_funds_rate)

    #   32310-CALCULATE-RESERVE-REQUIREMENT.
    #       COMPUTE WS-RESERVE-REQUIREMENT =
    #          WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS * WS-RESERVE-RATIO.

def calculate_reserve_requirement(treasury_data
