    def update_cfp_document(self):
        """33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT."""
        self.WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE = date.today()  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CFP-UPDATE-DATE
        self.CFP_OVERALL_STATUS = self.WS_CFP_STATUS  # MOVE WS-CFP-STATUS TO CFP-OVERALL-STATUS
        self.CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES = self.WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING  # MOVE WS-AVAILABLE-FUNDING TO CFP-TOTAL-SOURCES
        self.CFP_STRESS_NEEDS = self.WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS  # MOVE WS-STRESSED-OUTFLOWS TO CFP-STRESS-NEEDS
        self.rewrite_cfp_record(WsCfpDocument(cfp_overall_status = self.CFP_OVERALL_STATUS, 
                                              cfp_total_sources = self.CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES, 
                                              cfp_stress_needs = self.CFP_STRESS_NEEDS))# REWRITE CFP-RECORD FROM WS-CFP-DOCUMENT

    def capital_management(self):
        """34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.calculate_capital_ratios()  # PERFORM 34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS
        self.risk_weighted_assets()  # PERFORM 34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.capital_planning()  # PERFORM 34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING
        self.stress_testing()  # PERFORM 34400-STRESS-TESTING

    def calculate_capital_ratios(self):
        """34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS."""
        self.calculate_tier1()  # PERFORM 34110-CALCULATE-TIER1
        self.calculate_tier2()  # PERFORM 34120-CALCULATE-TIER2
        self.calculate_ratios()  # PERFORM 34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS

    def calculate_tier1(self):
        """34110-CALCULATE-TIER1."""
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_COMMON_STOCK  # ADD WS-COMMON-STOCK TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS  # ADD WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_AOCI  # ADD WS-AOCI TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_GOODWILL  # SUBTRACT WS-GOODWILL FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_INTANGIBLES  # SUBTRACT WS-INTANGIBLES FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_DTA_DEDUCTION  # SUBTRACT WS-DTA-DEDUCTION FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL

    def calculate_tier2(self):
        """34120-CALCULATE-TIER2."""
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_SUB_DEBT  # ADD WS-SUB-DEBT TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE  # ADD WS-ALLL-ELIGIBLE TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL  # COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL = WS-TIER1-CAPITAL + WS-TIER2-CAPITAL

    def calculate_ratios(self):
        """34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS."""
        if self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS > 0:  # IF WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS > 0
            self.WS_CET1_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-CET1-RATIO = (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
            self.WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO = (WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
        if self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:  # IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
            self.WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-LEVERAGE-RATIO = (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100

    def risk_weighted_assets(self):
        """34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.credit_rwa()  # PERFORM 34210-CREDIT-RWA
        self.market_rwa()  # PERFORM 34220-MARKET-RWA
        self.operational_rwa()  # PERFORM 34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA

    def credit_rwa(self):
        """34210-CREDIT-RWA."""
        self.WS_CASH_RWA = self.WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")  # COMPUTE WS-CASH-RWA = WS-CASH-POSITION * 0.00
        self.WS_GOVT_RWA = self.WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00")  # COMPUTE WS-GOVT-RWA = WS-GOVT-SECURITIES * 0.00
        self.WS_BANK_RWA = self.WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20")  # COMPUTE WS-BANK-RWA = WS-BANK-DEPOSITS * 0.20
        self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA = self.WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50")  # COMPUTE WS-MORTGAGE-RWA = WS-RESIDENTIAL-MORTGAGES * 0.50
        self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA = self.WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")  # COMPUTE WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA = WS-COMMERCIAL-LOANS * 1.00
        self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA = self.WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")  # COMPUTE WS-CONSUMER-RWA = WS-CONSUMER-LOANS * 1.00
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CASH_RWA  # ADD WS-CASH-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_GOVT_RWA  # ADD WS-GOVT-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_BANK_RWA  # ADD WS-BANK-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA  # ADD WS-MORTGAGE-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA  # ADD WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA  # ADD WS-CONSUMER-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def market_rwa(self):
        """34220-MARKET-RWA."""
        self.WS_MARKET_RWA = self.WS_TRADING_ASSETS * self.WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR  # COMPUTE WS-MARKET-RWA = WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-MARKET-RISK-FACTOR
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MARKET_RWA  # ADD WS-MARKET-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def operational_rwa(self):
        """34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA."""
        self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA = self.WS_GROSS_INCOME * self.WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR * Decimal("12.5")  # COMPUTE WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA = WS-GROSS-INCOME * WS-OPERATIONAL-FACTOR * 12.5
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA  # ADD WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def capital_planning(self):
        """34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING."""
        self.project_capital_needs()  # PERFORM 34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS
        self.identify_capital_actions()  # PERFORM 34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS
        self.update_capital_plan()  # PERFORM 34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN

    def project_capital_needs(self):
        """34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS."""
        self.WS_PROJECTED_RWA = self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS * (1 + self.WS_GROWTH_RATE)  # COMPUTE WS-PROJECTED-RWA = WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS * (1 + WS-GROWTH-RATE)
        self.WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL = self.WS_PROJECTED_RWA * self.WS_TARGET_RAT

def is_zeroes(value: Decimal) -> bool:
    """Check if a decimal is equal to zero."""
    return value == Decimal("0.00")

def current_date() -> date:
    """Return the current date."""
    return date.today()

def random_number() -> int:
    """Return a random integer."""
    import random
    return random.randint(0, 99999999999)

def integer_of_date(date_value: date) -> int:
    """Placeholder for date to integer conversion."""
    import datetime
    date_format = '%Y-%m-%d'
    date_object = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(date_value), date_format).date()
    start_date = datetime.date(1900, 1, 1)
    return (date_object - start_date).days + 2

def string(*args, delimiter: str = " ") -> str:
    """Implements the COBOL STRING statement."""
    parts = []
    for arg in args:
        if isinstance(arg, (int, float, Decimal)):
            parts.append(str(arg))  # Convert numeric values to strings
        elif isinstance(arg, str):
            parts.append(arg)
    return delimiter.join(parts)

    # Assuming a write function exists to handle this.
    #write_recon_exception_record(WS_RECON_EXCEPTION)
    RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION

def perform_37300_intercompany_recon():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON"""
    perform_37310_load_ic_balances()
    perform_37320_match_ic_pairs()
    perform_37330_report_ic_differences()

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    global WS_IC_ARRAY
    global INTERCOMPANY_FILE_RECORD
    global INTERCOMPANY_FILE
    
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        # Placeholder: Read INTERCOMPANY-FILE into WS-IC-BALANCE
        try:
            INTERCOMPANY_FILE_RECORD = "record" # Read from file
            # Assuming a read function exists to handle reading from file
            #INTERCOMPANY_FILE_RECORD = read_intercompany_file()
            
            WS_IC_BALANCE = WS_IC_BALANCE() # Assuming the file returns WS_IC_BALANCE attributes
            WS_IC_BALANCE.IC_FROM_ENTITY = INTERCOMPANY_FILE_RECORD
            WS_IC_BALANCE.IC_TO_ENTITY = INTERCOMPANY_FILE_RECORD
            WS_IC_BALANCE.IC_AMOUNT = Decimal("100")
            
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            if WS_IC_COUNT <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY):
                WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT-1] = WS_IC_BALANCE
            else:
                print("WS_IC_COUNT exceeds array size")
                WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        except StopIteration:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS"""
    global WS_IC_IDX
    global WS_IC_COUNT

    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART"""
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM
    global WS_SEARCH_TO
    global WS_IC_IDX
    global WS_IC_IDX2
    global WS_IC_COUNT
    global WS_IC_DIFF

    # Assuming IC_FROM_ENTITY and IC_TO_ENTITY are now attributes of elements in WS_IC_ARRAY
    # Accessed using WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_FROM_ENTITY to account for Python's 0-based indexing

    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_FROM_ENTITY  
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_TO_ENTITY

    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:

        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_AMOUNT
                if not is_zeroes(WS_IC_DIFF):
                    perform_37326_log_ic_diff()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF"""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM
    global WS_SEARCH_TO
    global WS_IC_DIFF
    global IC_DIFF_RECORD
    global IC_DIFF_RECORD_OBJ

    WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    
    IC_DIFF_RECORD = WS_IC_DIFF_REC # Assuming write to file function exists

def perform_38410_move_to_archive():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD
    global WS_ARCHIVE_DATE
    global AUDIT_FILE
    global ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        # Placeholder: Read AUDIT-FILE into WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        try:
            AUDIT_FILE_RECORD = "record"
            if current_date() < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD = AUDIT_FILE_RECORD
                # Placeholder: Delete AUDIT-FILE record
        except StopIteration:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def perform_39100_collect_metrics():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39100-COLLECT-METRICS"""
    perform_39110_cpu_metrics()
    perform_39120_memory_metrics()
    perform_39130_io_metrics()
    perform_39140_transaction_metrics()

def perform_39110_cpu_metrics():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39110-CPU-METRICS"""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION
    global WS_CPU_ALERT

    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu()
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

def perform_39120_memory_metrics():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39120-MEMORY-METRICS"""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION
    global WS_MEMORY_ALERT

    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION = getmem()
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION > 85:
        WS_MEMORY_ALERT = 'Y'

def perform_39130_io_metrics():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39130-IO-METRICS"""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME
    global WS_IO_THRESHOLD
    global WS_IO_ALERT

    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = getio()
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > WS_IO_THRESHOLD:
        WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y'

def perform_39140_transaction_metrics():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS"""
    global WS_TPS
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE
    global WS_TRANS_COUNT
    global WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS
    global WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME

    if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS != 0:
        WS_TPS = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) / Decimal(WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS)
    else:
        WS_TPS = Decimal("0.00") # Avoid division by zero

    if WS_TRANS_COUNT != 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT)
    else:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal("0.00") # Avoid division by zero

def perform_39200_analyze_performance():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE"""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE
    global WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD
    global WS_PERF_DEGRADED
    global WS_TPS
    global WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD
    global WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'

def perform_39300_generate_alerts():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39300-GENERATE-ALERTS"""
    global WS_CPU_ALERT
    global WS_MEMORY_ALERT
    global WS_PERF_DEGRADED

    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        perform_39310_send_cpu_alert()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        perform_39320_send_memory_alert()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        perform_39330_send_perf_alert()

def perform_39310_send_cpu_alert():
    """COBOL: PERFORM 39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT"""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    global WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL
    global WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'HIGH-CPU'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = string('ALERT: CPU utilization at ', str(WS_CPU_UTILIZATION), '%')
    send_notification() # 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION
